+
+// Function to filter destinations dynamically in Tab 1
+function filterDestinations(category) {
+    // 1. Reset all filter button styles
+    document.querySelectorAll('.dest-filter-btn').forEach(btn => {
+        btn.classList.remove('bg-teal-600', 'text-white', 'shadow-md');
+        btn.classList.add('bg-white', 'text-gray-600', 'border-gray-200');
+    });
+    
+    // 2. Find and style the active filter button
+    const activeBtn = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.dest-filter-btn')).find(btn => {
+        return btn.getAttribute('onclick').includes(`'${category}'`);
+    });
+    if (activeBtn) {
+        activeBtn.classList.remove('bg-white', 'text-gray-600', 'border-gray-200');
+        activeBtn.classList.add('bg-teal-600', 'text-white', 'shadow-md');
+    }
+    
+    // 3. Filter the cards with elegant transitions
+    const cards = document.querySelectorAll('#destinations-container .destination-card');
+    cards.forEach(card => {
+        const cardCat = card.getAttribute('data-category');
+        if (category === 'all' || cardCat === category) {
+            card.classList.remove('hidden');
+            // Re-trigger scroll reveal transitions for filtered cards
+            setTimeout(() => {
+                card.classList.add('visible');
+            }, 50);
+        } else {
+            card.classList.add('hidden');
+        }
+    });
+}
+
+// Function to filter dining & cafe cards dynamically in Tab 4
+function filterFood(category) {
+    // 1. Reset all food filter button styles
+    document.querySelectorAll('.food-filter-btn').forEach(btn => {
+        btn.classList.remove('bg-teal-600', 'text-white', 'shadow-md');
+        btn.classList.add('bg-white', 'text-gray-600', 'border-gray-200');
+    });
+    
+    // 2. Find and style the active filter button
+    const activeBtn = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.food-filter-btn')).find(btn => {
+        return btn.getAttribute('onclick').includes(`'${category}'`);
+    });
+    if (activeBtn) {
+        activeBtn.classList.remove('bg-white', 'text-gray-600', 'border-gray-200');
+        activeBtn.classList.add('bg-teal-600', 'text-white', 'shadow-md');
+    }
+    
+    // 3. Filter the cards with smooth display transitions
+    const cards = document.querySelectorAll('#food-container .food-card');
+    cards.forEach(card => {
+        const cardCat = card.getAttribute('data-category');
+        if (category === 'all' || cardCat === category) {
+            card.classList.remove('hidden');
+            setTimeout(() => {
+                card.classList.add('visible');
+            }, 50);
+        } else {
+            card.classList.add('hidden');
+        }
+    });
